- **[WSP-3R7K]** Tool-written artifacts aren't ignored in workspaces created before `init-workspace` (per-tool local `.gitignore` drop)
  `effort: S · impact: S · area: onboarding/install · source: user · added: 2026-06-17 · status: open · stage: ready · related: WS-BOOTSTRAP · refs: incoming-bugs/2026-06-14-song-workspace-gitignore-misses-tool-generated-artifacts.md`

  Hygiene/onboarding (severity L), the **residual half** of the song-workspace gitignore report after WS-BOOTSTRAP shipped. WS-BOOTSTRAP (`hallucinote init-workspace`) makes a *freshly-bootstrapped* workspace ship an idempotent `.gitignore` managed block (`*.db*`, `**/captures/`, `**/analysis/`, `**/.last-*-push.json`, `**/captured_session.json.bak`, `*.als*`, pyc) — but workspaces created **before** that bootstrap (or by a bare `git init` outside the skill) don't retroactively get the block, so they still surface tool-written regenerable noise as committable: `songs/*/.last-notes-push.json` (a notes-push state cache the rule missed, sibling to the already-ignored `.last-push-state.json`), `**/analysis/*.json` (MixReport outputs from the render/analyze path — swell had 13 staged), `**/captured_session.json.bak` (the snapshot backup). The durable per-tool fix (report direction #2): have each generating tool drop a local `.gitignore` in the directory it writes to (e.g. `analysis/.gitignore` = `*`, the way `captures/` could self-ignore), so the ignore travels with the artifact regardless of when/how the workspace was created. **Verifiable signal:** the render/analyze path writes `analysis/.gitignore` (`*`) when it creates `analysis/`, and the notes-push + snapshot paths self-ignore their state/backup files — a pre-bootstrap workspace stops surfacing these as committable without any manual root-`.gitignore` edit. (song-workspace gitignore dogfood, user, 2026-06-17)

## Promoted

- **[SMP-7K2D]** Sample-instrument + the playback-parameter model — author a Simpler/Sampler with an assigned sample file from build.py/DB (the swell buried-"we" keystone primitive; absorbs the cluster)
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: clip/audio · source: user · added: 2026-06-13 · status: promoted · stage: design · related: ENV-4S2K, AUD-7R3M, AST-6D3K, CLP-AUD2, AUD-1M4V · refs: .prawduct/artifacts/plans/SMP-7K2D/design.md, .prawduct/artifacts/plans/SMP-7K2D/build-plan.md, songs/swell/decisions/17-buried-we-centerpiece.md · reviewed: 2026-06-13`

  **PROMOTED + re-triaged to design (user, 2026-06-13).** Now has a `design.md` + `build-plan.md` (`.prawduct/artifacts/plans/SMP-7K2D/`); moved to ## Promoted as the **cluster keystone**. The design **collapses the whole swell buried-"we" cluster onto a single principle** — *there is ONE immutable source asset; window/gain/pitch/warp AND reverse are declared **playback parameters**, materialized at push, never a duplicated file* — and shrinks the cluster substantially. The DB surface reduces to **one nullable column** (`devices.audio_file`, the sample assignment — the *one* genuinely-missing primitive) + its snapshot encoding + push materialization; window/reverse/pitch/gain are existing Simpler/Sampler `device_parameters`. Per the design's per-item disposition: **ENV-4S2K** narrows to a confirmation test + ordering doc (its sample-accurate path already ships), **AUD-7R3M** reframes as `clips.reverse` / a `Reverse` device-param (a playback parameter, not a standalone capability), **AST-6D3K** is dropped (its transforms are already playback params; only a possible hidden push-time materialization fallback survives, tracked under this plan's chunk 4). Build-plan chunks 1/2/5 are buildable now (no-Live); 0/3/4 gate on the capability probe. See design §"Per-item disposition" + §"The capability probe".

  **The keystone of the swell buried-"we" centerpiece cluster** (4 decision-free primitives the centerpiece needs; siblings: **ENV-4S2K** sample-accurate device-param clip envelopes, **AUD-7R3M** audio reverse/fold, **AST-6D3K** derived-asset pipeline). Full report: `incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-12-sample-instrument-authoring-simpler-sampler.md`. Engine 0.9.0, hallucinote-mcp 0.1.0, Live 12.x.

  **The one missing primitive.** There is **no way to author a sample-instrument with an assigned sample file from `build.py`/the DB** (verified: no `simpler`/`sampler`/`load_sample`/`set_sample` authoring path in `src/` or `hallucinote_mcp/` — instruments can be *loaded* at MCP runtime but the sample assignment isn't part of the DB source-of-truth, so it doesn't survive a rebuild/push). Requested: a **sample-instrument part type** — a track whose instrument is a Simpler (or Sampler) with an **assigned `audio_file`** (reuse the existing clip-side `audio_file` resolution + `assets/` convention in `paths.py`). On push: load the device, point it at the resolved sample, materialize it like any other instrument; the existing `device_parameter` + `mixer_volume` envelope push then drives the rest.

  **The high-leverage finding (probed in a scratch Live set, 2026-06-12) — supersedes the audio-clip-push framing.** **Simpler** exposes automatable windowing (`S Start`/`S Length`, 0.0–1.0 continuous DeviceParameters — settable confirmed `S Start=0.5`→`"50.0 %"`) but **no** Reverse. **Sampler** exposes automatable `Reverse` (enum Off/On) but **no** `S Start`/`S Length` window. So per-phrase windowing is automatable **today** on the existing push surface (MIDI trigger notes + `device_parameter` envelopes + `mixer_volume` aura) — **no CLP-AUD2 / audio-clip push required** for swell. (The heavier CLP-AUD2 audio-clip path stays the right tool for true arrangement audio / non-instrument material — keep it on its existing roadmap; this primitive is the critical path here, on machinery that already ships.)

  **Scope boundary — framework owns decision-free primitives ONLY.** The **windowed-reveal generator** (center-pinning, `width = f(breath)`, the forward-mirror fold, the summit release) is deliberately NOT a framework ask — it encodes per-song musical decisions and lives in `songs/swell/breath.py` (great-art-not-software: helpers remove bookkeeping, never make the musical decision). The only promotable seam is the thin decision-free translation *(window start/length, reverse, level) per phrase → trigger note + device-parameter envelopes* — promote on the **rule of three**, not pre-emptively. **Why `stage: requirements`:** the DB schema + push materialization for "instrument-with-assigned-sample" needs a spec (where the sample assignment lives in the device-chain model, how it composes with the device-load push phase, how it reuses `audio_file`/`assets/` resolution). **Verifiable signal:** a `build.py` authoring a Simpler-with-assigned-sample part + `S Start`/`S Length` device-parameter envelopes + a volume aura **pushes to Live and plays the windowed, center-pinned reveal audibly, idempotently** — with no audio-clip-push dependency. **Sized:** medium (schema + push device-load-with-sample + a decision-record on placement). (swell buried-"we" centerpiece cluster, user, 2026-06-12)

## Archive

Closed investigations — no fix possible / structural-close on Ableton's roadmap. Kept for search so a future scrub doesn't re-open them without new evidence. Status `dropped` = investigated and intentionally not pursued; `shipped` = built and closed.

- **[MCP-4T6Y]** SUPERSEDED — Long-running actions (render, analyze, ensure_loaded on 25 surfaces) blow the MCP bridge call timeout while work continues server-side → make them ASYNC + a `/render-analyze` skill
  `effort: M · impact: L · area: mcp · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-10 · status: dropped · stage: design · related: MCP-9R3T, MCP-5N8K, DEV-6T2W · closes: MCP-9R3T, MCP-5N8K · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-10-swell-first-compose-friction.md, incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-analyze-action-exceeds-60s-mcp-timeout-multi-stem.md, incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-async-render-capability-and-skill-design.md, incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-14-render-analyze-60s-false-failure-dominant-tax.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  **SUPERSEDED 2026-06-18 — split into MCP-9R3T (async render) + MCP-5N8K (async analyze)**, both at `impact: H`, per a user request to track render and analyze as two HIGH-priority async fixes. The full converged design below was preserved into both children (the shared async `start`→`status`→completion-notify pattern + the follow-on `/render-analyze` skill, which depends on both). Kept here for search/history; do NOT pick this — pick the two children. Original design text follows.

  From the swell friction log (item 7). Long-running actions — `ensure_loaded` across 25 surfaces, a full render — exceed the MCP bridge's read window; the call "fails" client-side while the work continues server-side, leaving the agent blind to completion. Needs a progress/async pattern (job handle + poll) or chunked per-track ensure so each call fits the window. Related to DEV-6T2W (the 15s server-side main-thread ceiling, archived).

  **Re-confirmed + widened (swell mix dogfood, 2026-06-13, two reports).** The ceiling is now **60s** (raised from the original 15s) and is **per-MCP-call, global** — and it is confirmed to hit **`analyze` AND `render`** AND to **block unrelated calls** (`session(info)` timed out at 60s simply because the server was busy driving a long action). Specifics: (a) `ableton_analysis(action='analyze')` on a 23-surface song (loudness+attribution+masking+timing+cross-rhythm, all auto-enabled when sections are declared) exceeds 60s and **no report is written** — yet the *same* handler called in-process from Python with a longer budget completes cleanly and writes the report, so the DSP is fine; the bottleneck is purely the transport ceiling. (b) `ableton_render` is **inherently realtime/multi-minute** (≈98s playback for 190 beats + ring-out) — a 60s cap can essentially never fit a real render, yet renders **do finish + write a well-formed `manifest.json` (status: ok)**: the caller gets a **FALSE FAILURE** (error returned, work actually succeeded) with **no completion signal**, forcing a filesystem poll for `manifest.json`. This makes the async fix not nice-to-have but **necessary for `render` to be usable at all** without polling workarounds.

  **The robust fix = make `render` + `analyze` ASYNC** (the two reports' converged design): split into `start` (returns immediately: `{render_id, captures_dir, expected_stop_beat, eta_seconds}` — tiny payload, never holds the socket for the realtime duration) → `status(render_id)` (tiny: `{state: running|done|failed, progress_beats, captures_dir, error?}`, no file lists) → on completion the server writes the existing `manifest.json` sentinel **and** emits a completion event the host can surface as a **background-task notification** (same channel Claude Code uses for backgrounded Bash) so the agent is *woken on done* instead of burning turns polling. `start` must not block other MCP calls — if Live's single-threaded LOM forbids true concurrency during a transport pass, at minimum reject/queue concurrent calls with an instant `{busy: true, render_id}` rather than hanging them to a 60s timeout (what `session(info)` did mid-render). Mitigations (action-scoped timeout bump; DSP parallelization) only defer the problem to larger songs — async is the real fix since analysis time is unbounded in track count.

  **Plus a `/render-analyze` skill** (the least-context-polluting path): `/render-analyze [start_beat] [stop_beat] [--compare <seq>]` orchestrates render→wait-on-completion→analyze entirely OUT of the agent's main context and returns to the agent **only** the MixReport `summary` (true-peak, overshoot count, per-section count, out-of-tolerance reverbs) + `report_path` + an optional one-line delta vs `--compare`. Full per-stem/per-band JSON stays on disk for selective reads. Net agent context for a full verify cycle: one skill call in, one ~6-line summary out (vs today's timed-out render error + filesystem poll + manual in-process analyze + hand-written extraction scripts).

  **Why impact L (bumped from M):** analysis cost scales with surface count (per-stem passes) and the cross-surface masking/attribution terms (O(stems)→O(stems²)), and most *finished* songs are 15–25 tracks — so this hits **essentially every song at mix-review time**, precisely when the tool is most needed; not a swell edge case. *(Children re-rated `impact: H` per the user's 2026-06-18 request.)*

  **Verifiable signal:** `render` and `analyze` run as async actions (`start`→`{render_id,eta}`; `status` poll; completion surfaced via the host background-task notification, no false-failure / no socket held for the realtime duration), a concurrent `session(info)` during a long action does not hang to timeout, and a `/render-analyze` skill returns ONLY the MixReport summary + report_path (no WAV lists / full JSON in agent context). Interim that works today: call `render` (ignore the false failure), background a bash loop waiting on `manifest.json`, then run `analyze` in-process.

- **[PSH-8K3D]** `apply_push_results` halts the devices phase on an unknown `device_param_override` result kind — a full from-scratch push is impossible for any song with a `value_raw` nested override
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: push · source: user · added: 2026-06-18 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PR #180 (787aeeb) · related: DEV-4P7R, SNP-2H9F · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-18-push-apply-unknown-device_param_override-result-kind-halts-devices-phase.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  HIGH-severity push-side correctness bug (surfaced rebuilding `swell` into a fresh blank Live set, 2026-06-18). `sync/push/plan.py::apply_push_results` switches on a result-key `kind` and has **no case** for `device_param_override` — the kind the devices phase emits when a planned `value_raw` override is applied and its realized value comes back. The device *loads* succeed; the **result-apply** step then `raise ValueError("unknown push result key kind 'device_param_override' …")` (~plan.py:496), HALTING the devices phase. Effect: a **full push from scratch is impossible** for any song whose `captured_session.json` declares a `value_raw` device-param override (the swell Voice Lead `Synth Vox Ai` rack `LFO 1 S. Rate=8.0`) — the song never finishes materializing (no routing / envelopes / automation / arrangement / cues). This is the **push-apply** sibling of the param-override carry-side bugs (DEV-4P7R, SNP-2H9F): even a well-formed override that pushes correctly halts the run because the *result* can't be applied. **Fix (shipped PR #180):** added `"device_param_override"` to `_ACK_ONLY_KINDS` in `sync/push/plan.py` (the realized value needs no DB write — it's an ack); after the fix swell's full push completed (devices 174/174 ok). **Carried-forward open question — RESOLVED (shipped PR #180, 2026-06-18):** ack-only is correct. The independent Critic confirmed there is no reconcile-back to do — the `value_raw` override value originates in the snapshot/DB (the author's declared intent), so there is no Live-side index to reconcile back into the source (same as its sibling `device_parameter`); the realized result is just an acknowledgement. **Verifiable signal:** a from-scratch full push of a song carrying a `value_raw` override completes the devices phase (and all downstream phases) without raising on the `device_param_override` result kind. (swell rebuild-reliability cluster, user, 2026-06-18)

- **[SYN-7N4K]** `replay_capture` nulls authored sidechain sources every build — converger idempotency permanently broken for any song with a build.py-authored sidechain
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: sync · source: user · added: 2026-06-18 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PR #180 (787aeeb) · related: BAK-3M9T, SDC-7K3M · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-17-replay-capture-nulls-sidechain-source-breaking-converger-idempotency.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  Medium-severity converger-discipline bug (surfaced dogfooding `swell`, 2026-06-17). The load-bearing "re-run = zero net state-change events" converger promise (and its guard test `test_build_is_idempotent_state_converger`) is **un-satisfiable** for any song that authors a sidechain source — so the idempotency test can never pass and stops catching the real non-convergence it exists to catch. **Two-step bind:** (1) `captured_session.json` can't carry a sidechain source (the known SDC-7K3M / BAK-3M9T author+push-only gap), so the snapshot has no source field; (2) `replay_capture` therefore emits `device_sidechain_set(source=null)` for each Compressor on every build, *actively clearing* the source the previous build authored; (3) `_author_sidechains` then re-sets the real source. Net `<real> → null → <real>` per sidechain = 2 guaranteed state-change events × N sidechains, forever (swell: 5 sidechains → 10 extra events per build, `730 == 720`). This is the canonical "song with a mix-pass sidechain" shape (every `/mix-sidechain` + decisions/19 song), so the guard test is dead-on-arrival for all of them, training authors to ignore a red test. The song's *materialized* state is correct; only the converger-idempotency invariant is broken. **Fix (shipped PR #180):** make replay treat snapshot silence as no-opinion — when the snapshot carries no sidechain source for a device, `replay_capture` leaves the existing DB source as-is (absent ≠ null), the smaller more general fix than closing SDC-7K3M's capture gap. **Verifiable signal:** a song with N build.py-authored sidechains passes `test_build_is_idempotent_state_converger` (re-running build produces 0 extra state-change events) without capturing the source into the snapshot. **Accepted cost (shipped PR #180, documented in README Known issues + CHANGELOG):** because the fix treats snapshot silence as no-opinion (absent ≠ null), the snapshot can no longer express "remove this sidechain" — clearing/removing a sidechain in Live needs a full rebuild, not an incremental converge. (swell rebuild-reliability cluster, user, 2026-06-18)

- **[IDX-5W2P]** `reindex_markdown` hard-fails the whole corpus on a frontmatter-less `decisions/*.md` — silently breaking `/song-context` decisions FTS
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: song-context/indexing · source: user · added: 2026-06-18 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PR #180 (787aeeb) · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-17-reindex-markdown-hard-fails-corpus-on-frontmatterless-decisions.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  Low-medium-severity silent-search-degradation bug (surfaced dogfooding `swell`, 2026-06-17). `load_markdown_doc` (`src/hallucinote/markdown_refs.py:343`) raises on the first file with no `---` frontmatter, and `reindex_corpus` (`:464`) parses the corpus in a list comprehension (`docs = [load_markdown_doc(p, …) for p in corpus]`) — so **one malformed file aborts the entire corpus**, indexing nothing. Scope: in swell all 23 `decisions/*.md` start with `# NN — Title` and carry NO frontmatter (only the 7 `annotations/*.md` do); the reindexer globs both and dies on `decisions/01`, so swell's decisions corpus has likely **never** been FTS-indexed and `/song-context` (which advertises "decisions + annotations … FTS5-indexed") silently returns nothing from decisions. Any song scaffolded the same way is affected. Mismatch: the decisions-authoring convention emits frontmatter-less `# heading` decisions, but the indexer requires frontmatter. **Fix (shipped PR #180) — both cheap, independent halves:** (1) **resilience** — `reindex_corpus` skip-and-warn per file so one unparseable doc is counted/reported and the rest still index (the difference between "degraded" and "dead"); (2) **tolerance** — `load_markdown_doc` treats a missing-frontmatter file as empty metadata + indexes the body (title from the first `# ` heading), since decisions are legitimately frontmatter-optional. **Verifiable signal:** running `reindex_markdown` over a song whose `decisions/*.md` lack frontmatter indexes every parseable doc (decisions FTS-searchable via `/song-context`) and warns-without-aborting on any genuinely unparseable file. **Critic-caught refinement (shipped PR #180):** the frontmatter tolerance is **decisions-only** — `annotations/*.md` and `attempts/*.md` still require frontmatter, because an attempt can't satisfy its required `outcome`/`resolution` fields from a bare body. (swell rebuild-reliability cluster, user, 2026-06-18)

- **[BAK-3M9T]** UMBRELLA: the live→source "bake" should be ONE turnkey command, not an expert-only multi-trap operation
  `effort: L · impact: L · area: snapshot/sync · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-13 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PR #178 (90214d3) · related: SDC-7K3M, SYN-9F2L, SNP-4K7M, AUD-2N6K, SNP-8R4K, DPP-7H2K · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-live-to-source-bake-should-be-turnkey.md, .prawduct/artifacts/plans/BAK-3M9T/requirements.md, .prawduct/artifacts/plans/BAK-3M9T/build-plan.md · reviewed: 2026-06-17`

  **The goal (swell mix dogfood, 2026-06-13; user: "we really want this to be easy to use").** Baking a Live mix back into source should be ONE reliable command — *change the mix in Live → run one thing → `captured_session.json` updates → `python build.py` + push reproduces it* — with no hand-editing and no format lore. Today it is an **expert-only, multi-trap operation**: the agent had to bypass `/song-snapshot` entirely and hand-author the snapshot JSON in Python, learning each sharp edge by hitting it.

  **This is the umbrella that rolls up the bake traps — each a child item.** The traps that silently break the bake today:
  1. **Analyzer pollution** — `/song-snapshot` captures the `HallucinoteAnalyzer` devices (render leaves them in every chain, sometimes interleaved). → **SNP-8R4K**.
  2. **Device ordering** — devices loaded after a render land *after* the analyzer, so per-stem captures under-measure them. → **SNP-8R4K** (same root).
  3. **`params_dialed` value-vs-normalized silent-wrong-dial** — probes report a param's value in its OWN range (Threshold −10 dB, Transients 0.30 in −1..1), but `set_device_parameter` wants `value_normalized ∈ [0,1]`; passing the wrong one either errors OR (worse, for a −1..1 param whose value lands in [0,1]) is **silently accepted and dials the WRONG value**, with no probe field handing back the true 0–1 normalized. → **SYN-9F2L**. Fix direction: store BOTH the true 0–1 normalized AND the display, push picks the safe one per param.
  4. **Display-vs-normalized monotonicity at push** — push prefers `value_display`, which can't be inverted for non-monotonic params (Hz↔kHz, ms↔s); so freq/rate params must bake normalized-only while non-0–1 monotonic params (dB) must bake as a display string — opposite rules, no guardrail. → **DPP-7H2K**.
  5. **Enum params** — must carry `value_items`; unclear they round-trip through push without it.
  6. **Return naming / send keys** — returns stored slot-stripped ('Motion', not Live's 'E-Motion'); sends must key to the stripped name (easy to get wrong by copying Live's displayed name).
  7. **Sidechain sources don't model at all** without a dedicated path → silently dropped. → **SDC-7K3M** (author+push done; pull-capture remaining).

  **The umbrella's deliverable:** a `/song-snapshot` (or `bake` action) that **just-works post-render** — filters analyzer devices, captures each dialed param **losslessly** (store BOTH the true 0–1 normalized AND the display; push picks the safe one per param), handles enums + return-name stripping, and **WARNS (never silently drops)** anything unmodelable (e.g. "N sidechains not captured; here's the re-apply list"). Plus a probe that returns the **true 0–1 normalized** alongside value/display so authoring tools never guess a param's range, and a `diff` that's safe to trust for confirm/overwrite (it exists; the capture side just needs to stop polluting it). Net: the agent should never have to know `params_dialed` internals or monotonicity rules to persist a mix. **Why `stage: requirements`:** the unified bake surface composes several children whose individual fixes are themselves at various stages — needs a requirements pass settling the one-command UX + the per-param lossless-capture contract before code. **Verifiable signal:** one command, run post-render, refreshes `captured_session.json` such that `build.py` + push reproduces the dialed mix (analyzer-free, params lossless, enums + return-naming handled), and every unmodelable element surfaces as a warning with a re-apply list rather than a silent drop. (swell bake turnkey umbrella, user, 2026-06-13)

  **REQUIREMENTS PASS COMPLETE (2026-06-17 — see `.prawduct/artifacts/plans/BAK-3M9T/requirements.md`).** Audit found **6/7 traps already shipped**: analyzer pollution + device ordering (**SNP-8R4K**), value-vs-normalized + monotonicity + lossless capture (**SYN-9F2L** + **DEV-4P7R** + **DPP-7H2K**), enums, and return-naming. The residual is **NOT a missing capability — it is a DURABILITY MISMATCH**: only `/song-snapshot` writes the *durable* target (`captured_session.json`), while `/ableton-pull` + `/snapshot-bake-recent-changes` write the *regenerable* DB, which `replay_capture` reverts on the next `build.py`. **DECISION (user): Option 1 — one durable bake.** Model sidechain in the snapshot, DELETE `/snapshot-bake-recent-changes` (redundant alias), and reframe `/ableton-pull` as build.py-staging. **Next:** `/prawduct:planning` for the build plan (chunks A–D in requirements.md). (requirements pass complete, user, 2026-06-17)

  **BUILD PLAN AUTHORED (2026-06-17 — see `.prawduct/artifacts/plans/BAK-3M9T/build-plan.md`). `stage: ready`.** 4 chunks: **01** model sidechain in snapshot (keystone vertical slice — no new DB column; reuses SDC-7K3M cols + push half); **02** warn + re-apply-list on unrepresentable sidechain; **03** DELETE `/snapshot-bake-recent-changes` (redundant alias) + clean refs; **04** reframe `/ableton-pull` as build.py-staging + one-bake doc. Build on a worktree off develop; Critic via independent Agents + `gh`. **Requirements Confidence: High.** (build plan authored, user, 2026-06-17)

  _Shipped 2026-06-17 (PR #178, squash 90214d3, merged to develop): one durable bake landed end-to-end. Sidechain source now round-trips through `captured_session.json` (ch01); capture warns on unrepresentable sources rather than silently dropping (ch02); `/snapshot-bake-recent-changes` deleted + refs redirected (ch03); `/ableton-pull` reframed as build.py-staging + the one-bake model documented (ch04). 4071 tests green; ch01 final Critic 0/0/2, cumulative Critic 0/0/5. Live operator round-trip (acceptance criterion 5) merged PENDING by user direction — recorded in operator-verification.md, re-verify when Live is open._

- **[DEV-4P7R]** `param_overrides` can't durably carry a quantized continuous param whose raw range ≠ [0,1] and whose display is non-monotonic
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: device/sync · source: user · added: 2026-06-17 · reviewed: 2026-06-17 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PR #177 (9e0a252) · related: SNP-2H9F, DPP-7H2K, DEV-9K7N · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-17-param-overrides-cannot-carry-quantized-nonunit-range-param.md`

  Residual facet of SNP-2H9F (PR #176), discovered while applying that fix to the swell `21 Voice Lead` case it was built for — **not a regression.** `param_overrides` (and `params_dialed`) can't durably carry a quantized continuous device-param whose raw range ≠ [0,1] AND whose display is non-monotonic (e.g. Wavetable `LFO S. Rate`: raw `8.0` → display `"1/2"`, range `[0,21]`). All three channels fail: the **display channel is refused at push** (`DisplayValueError`: non-monotonic); `value_normalized` is **clamped to [0,1] at storage** AND is **pushed as a RAW value anyway** (the push handler has no `value_normalized` kwarg — `value_type='continuous'` takes raw `value` in `[param.min, param.max]`), so neither continuous channel can express a raw-`8.0` target. Sibling of DPP-7H2K's non-monotonic-display refusal, but the distinguishing twist here is the **non-unit raw range** — the value lives outside `[0,1]` so the normalized channel can't carry it even unclamped, and the display channel is closed by monotonicity. **Proposed fix:** add an unclamped `value_raw` REAL column to `device_param_overrides` + `device_parameters`; a push branch that emits it as the raw `value`; capture/replay + pull auto-emit for the quantized-non-unit-range param class. **Verifiable signal:** a quantized continuous param with raw range ≠ [0,1] and non-monotonic display (Wavetable `LFO S. Rate` = `8.0`/`"1/2"`) round-trips DB→push→capture→DB durably via `value_raw`, surviving a from-scratch rebuild+push. (swell quantized-non-unit-range dogfood, user, 2026-06-17)

  _Shipped 2026-06-17 (PR #177, squash 9e0a252, merged to develop): `value_raw` channel for quantized non-[0,1] device params shipped end-to-end — schema + additive migration on `device_parameters` + `device_param_overrides`, mutators with exclusivity + dedup, push raw-before-display branch, capture/pull auto-emit for non-enum range ≠ [0,1] params, docs. Independent Critic found+fixed B1 (flatten dropped `value_raw` on the `/song-snapshot` path) + W1 (large-magnitude raw churn); verify-resolutions clean. 4057p/2s, +20 tests. Remaining (Live-gated, noted): the real swell `LFO S. Rate` rebuild durability proof needs the dev engine deployed._

- **[SNP-2H9F]** Snapshot can't durably carry a nested-device param override on a `preset_query` instrument
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: device · source: user · added: 2026-06-17 · reviewed: 2026-06-17 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PR #176 (2feb163) · related: DEV-9K7N, DEV-7K4H, SNP-4K7M · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-17-snapshot-cannot-carry-nested-param-override-on-preset-instrument.md, .prawduct/artifacts/plans/NODE-ADDR/snp-2h9f-slice.md`

  **BUILT 2026-06-17 on `feat/snp-2h9f-param-overrides`; merged to develop via PR #176 (squash 2feb163; Critic cumulative 0-blocking).** `param_overrides` shipped end-to-end: `device_param_overrides` table + `DEVICE_PARAM_OVERRIDES_REPLACED` event (tombstone-registered) + `replace_device_param_overrides`/`get_device_param_overrides`; `_replay_devices` apply-path + chains-XOR guard; `_emit_param_override_writes` node-addressed push (no chain creation); `preserve_preset_overrides` capture conversion; docs + 34 tests. Plan: `snp-2h9f-slice.md`. **Verifiable signal met** (capture→replay→DB→push round-trip, depth-2, fake-probe). **Fast-follows (deferred, not lost — noted here, no separate tracking warranted: each is contingent/no-op-until-triggered, not confirmed standalone work):** (1) **pull-symmetry** — `/ableton-pull` doesn't emit `param_overrides` (confirmed non-corrupting: it's a no-op on a stable preset device; a pulled nested tweak lands as transient `device_parameters` rather than a durable override); (2) **drum-rack-via-preset with authored per-chain props** — capture keeps the full `chains` dump + warns (param_overrides is device-params only); (3) bounded preset-reference cache (design §8 / Critic note b) if preset over-capture bloats. **Re-file as a linked item only if one of these surfaces as confirmed work** (e.g. preset over-capture actually bloats a snapshot in practice).

  Silent durability loss (severity M): a hand-tweaked nested param deep inside a rack loaded via `preset_query` is reverted on every from-scratch rebuild+push, with no warning — the agent/user believes a documented mix fix is durable while the rebuild silently restores the preset default. **This is the same witness** that DEV-7K4H/DEV-9K7N (NODE-ADDR Chunk B, the #17b durability gap) already record (swell `21 Voice Lead`: `Synth Vox Ai` → Wavetable `LFO 1 Sync` Free→Tempo + `LFO 1 S. Rate` 1/8→1/2, "SAVE the `.als` or lose it"; decisions/22 #8's "a normal rebuild+push won't reset it" is **wrong**). Filed as the **specific representation requirement** that NODE-ADDR's generic deep-addressing scope doesn't yet call out: **there is no clean snapshot shape for "load instrument X from its portable `preset_query`, then override nested param P."** Root cause (`src/hallucinote/capture.py`): `_replay_devices` accepts `preset_query` **XOR** a full `chains` dump — `preset_query` loads the preset (incl. un-parameterizable bits: the Wavetable waveform/samples) but only **top-level** `params_dialed` apply afterward (the loop sets params on the just-created top-level `device_id`, never a descendant; ~L168/L202/L228/L243); adding a `chains` array to a `preset_query` device **creates** new nested chains (`_replay_rack_chains` → `create_device_chain`, ~L292/L339) → duplication/conflict, not override; the full-capture refresh (`capture execute`) sidesteps it by **dropping** `preset_query` and dumping explicit `chains`+`params_dialed`, but a Wavetable's loaded **waveform is not a DeviceParameter** so the dump can't reproduce the timbre (replacing `preset_query` risks losing the core sound) and it bloats (swell's curated 52 KB snapshot → 1.3 MB full dump, 37k-line diff). The *read* exists (`compile_snapshot`/`assemble_snapshot_via_probes` already probe nested params at every depth, ~L64/L691) and the *apply primitive* exists (`M.create_device`/`M.set_device_parameter` accept a deep NodeAddr `path` — how the live MCP setter reaches `[{1,1},{1,1}]`); what's missing is the **authorable override representation + the replay apply-path that calls the setter for a preset device's descendants** (no `create_device_chain`). **Proposed fix:** add a `param_overrides` field to a `preset_query`/`preset_uri` device entry — a flat list of `{path, name, value[, normalized][, value_items]}` where `path` is the NodeAddr descent relative to the loaded instrument; replay resolves each by `path` (existing resolver) and `set_device_parameter` on it (no chain creation → no duplication, preset waveform/samples survive); `capture execute` emits `param_overrides` for a preset-seeded device's non-default nested params instead of dropping `preset_query`. **Why a distinct item, not buried in NODE-ADDR:** the `preset_query` XOR `chains` representation problem + the Wavetable-waveform-loss + snapshot-bloat tradeoffs are a concrete schema/replay design the umbrella's generic addressing scope doesn't capture — route to `/prawduct:planning` to fold the `param_overrides` shape into NODE-ADDR Chunk B (or its own slice). **Until shipped:** document the manual MCP re-apply as a per-song runbook step (swell's `voice-lfo-sync-rebuild-fragility` annotation), and/or warn at push time when a `preset_query` instrument had nested non-default params at last capture. **Verifiable signal:** a `preset_query` instrument with a `param_overrides` entry round-trips DB→push→capture→DB durably (the nested override survives a from-scratch rebuild) without dropping `preset_query` or duplicating chains. (swell nested-param dogfood, user, 2026-06-17)

  _Shipped 2026-06-17 (merged to develop via PR #176, squash 2feb163): `param_overrides` lets a `preset_query` instrument durably carry nested device-param overrides across a from-scratch rebuild+push. Three fast-follows deferred and noted on this entry (pull-symmetry, drum-rack-via-preset per-chain props, bounded preset-cache) — file a linked item if any becomes confirmed work._

- **[PSH-6W2J]** Note-content changes never reach arrangement clips — silent stale render/playback (HIGH severity correctness)
  `effort: M · impact: L · area: push · source: user · added: 2026-06-17 · reviewed: 2026-06-17 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: PSH-6W2J · related: ARR-6T8N · refs: incoming-bugs/2026-06-15-note-changes-never-reach-arrangement-clips.md`

  **HIGH-severity silent correctness bug** (surfaced dogfooding swell, 2026-06-15). After a song's arrangement clips are materialized (first push runs `duplicate_to_arrangement` and writes `arrangement_clip` links), rewriting a part's notes in `build.py` and re-pushing leaves the **arrangement** — what actually plays/renders — frozen at first-materialization. Every downstream signal reports success (push exit 0, `clips N/N ok`, DB correct, `/mix-review` runs clean) but the analysis reads audio of the OLD notes. Repro: swell timpani rewritten 30→303 notes, rebuilt, pushed via `push-notes --changed` / `--clip <id>` / full `execute --probe` (all report success); render unchanged across 4 passes; `ableton_probe get_notes_extended` on the arrangement clip showed 24 (old) notes while the DB clip had 303. **Root cause:** `push_notes` (and the `clips` phase) `replace_notes` on the **session** clip resolved via the `clip` link; the arrangement clip is a distinct copy made once by `plan_push_arrangement` → `duplicate_to_arrangement`, and `plan_push_arrangement` is idempotent on **existence** of the `arrangement_clip` link (`already_linked` skip) — it never compares note content of source vs arrangement copy, so a re-push with changed notes emits zero arrangement ops ("arrangement skipped (idempotent)"). The author burned ~4 render+analyze cycles (~45 min) chasing a "level-capped timpani" that was a stale arrangement; the only way to catch it is to probe arrangement-clip note counts directly, which no normal workflow does. **Fix directions (pick one):** (1) **best** — `push_notes` also rewrites the linked arrangement clip(s) (a note change to a clip with an `arrangement_clip` link must propagate via `replace_notes` on the arrangement clip or re-duplicate); (2) make `plan_push_arrangement`'s idempotency check **content-aware** — fingerprint source-clip note content vs the arrangement copy and re-emit `duplicate_to_arrangement` (clearing the stale clip + link) when they diverge; (3) **minimum** — emit a LOUD warning when `push_notes` updates a clip that has an `arrangement_clip` link ("session clip updated; arrangement copy now stale") + document the re-materialize path (silence is the worst part). Current workaround: clear the stale arrangement clips + their links, then re-run the arrangement phase (fiddly + undiscoverable). (swell dogfood, user, 2026-06-15)

  _Shipped 2026-06-17 (commit bda7bd4, change-log tag PSH-6W2J, status=merged): note edits now propagate to arrangement clips._

- **[AUD-8K2N]** Non-Max-for-Live audio analysis path for Standard-edition users (degraded, master-bus only)
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: audio · source: user · added: 2026-06-16 · reviewed: 2026-06-17 · status: shipped · stage: research · closed-by: docs/capability-truth.md (gap-explanation; build declined by design) · refs: docs/capability-truth.md, README.md (Status — Live edition note), ableton://guides/gaps · related: DEV-9C4L, AUD-4S8T, SNP-8R4K`

  **DECIDED out of scope by the user (2026-06-17):** there is **no non-M4L analysis path, by design.** The audio-feedback half (render → analysis → `/mix-review`) stays Max-for-Live-only; the degraded master-bus-only export/freeze path is **not built.** The only remaining obligation — **explain the gap to Standard-edition users** — is now SHIPPED: `docs/capability-truth.md` (the anti-hallucination spine read by the install handoff + `/song-new`) splits **Mix** into *authoring* (any edition) vs *measured review* (Max for Live only → use symbolic `/compose-review` on Standard); README Status, `/getting-started` step 2, and `/ableton-mcp-install` Step 3d already state it. Status=shipped (gap-explanation shipped; build declined by design). **Reopen only if the edition constraint changes** (e.g. Ableton ships analysis-capable Standard). (user, 2026-06-17)

  ---
  _Original investigation (preserved):_ Today the whole audio-feedback half (render → analysis → `/mix-review`) depends on the **HallucinoteAnalyzer**, a Max for Live device — so it is **Suite-only**. Standard-edition users get authoring + symbolic `/compose-review` + play-by-ear, but **no measured mix review**.

  **The opening.** The analysis math is pure Python (librosa on WAVs); only the per-surface **CAPTURE** is M4L. So a **non-M4L capture path** — Live audio export / freeze → WAV — could feed the *same* analysis **without Max**. But export/freeze can only capture the **master mix**, NOT per-stem taps, so it loses **per-stem masking attribution** (the rich part of `/mix-review`).

  **Scope honestly (a degraded, partial mix-review, NOT parity with the M4L path).** Standard users would gain loudness / master-bus measurement (the master attribution + reverb-verification surfaces that already run on the master capture), but NOT the per-stem masking attribution that makes `/mix-review` rich. The decision this item gates: **is the partial signal worth the build?** — i.e. does a master-only, no-attribution mix review meaningfully serve Standard users, or does the degradation make it misleading? Relates to **DEV-9C4L** (M4L bridge — the Suite-side escape hatch this would NOT use), **AUD-4S8T** (capture-stop bracketing — a non-M4L export would sidestep the per-surface length ramp entirely), and **SNP-8R4K** (analyzer-in-chain pollution — a no-M4L path has no analyzer to filter).

  **Stage: research** — the core question is a value/feasibility decision, not a coding task. A research/requirements pass establishes whether Live's export/freeze can produce an analysis-ready master WAV through the bridge (or whether it needs an out-of-band manual export), what the degraded MixReport looks like with attribution disabled, and whether the partial signal clears the bar. **Verifiable signal:** a decision-record (or requirements artifact) stating whether a non-M4L master-bus-only analysis path ships for Standard users — and if yes, what surfaces it includes (loudness / master attribution / reverb) vs. consciously omits (per-stem masking attribution); if no, the rationale. (user, 2026-06-16)

- **[ENV-5R2J]** Dedup the host-kind→push-route mapping — encoded twice (push planner + DB eligibility layer)
  `effort: S · impact: S · area: envelope · source: critic · added: 2026-06-11 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: ENV-7G4K · reviewed: 2026-06-14 · closed-by: PR #169 (c150572) → develop`

  From the ENV-7G4K cumulative Critic (note finding): the host-kind→push-route mapping is encoded in two places — `sync/push/envelopes.py` `_route_for_host_kind` and the DB-mutator eligibility layer in `db/mutations` — so the two partitions can drift independently. Dedup to one shared source of truth (chunk 02 already named `classify_envelope_route` as "the single partition source"; make that literally true by having both sites consume it). Low priority, code-health. **Verifiable signal:** exactly one module encodes the host-kind→route partition; `grep` for the mapping table finds a single definition, with `sync/push/envelopes.py` and `db/mutations` both importing it.

  **Sharpened (verify-first triage, 2026-06-13) — these are RELATED-BUT-DISTINCT concerns, not a trivial dedup.** The mutator gate (`db/mutations/devices.py`) encodes host-kind **ELIGIBILITY** (which host kinds may carry a device-param envelope at all); the planner (`sync/push/envelopes.py` `_route_for_host_kind`) encodes the **ROUTE partition** (given an eligible host, which push path it takes). They merely share the `{midi, master, group, audio}` host-kind set — they are not the same table. A true single-source dedup needs a **neutral shared module** both can import: having `db/mutations` import from `sync/push` is a layering inversion. So this is its own focused change (extract a neutral host-kind module, point both eligibility + route sites at it), NOT a quick bundle onto adjacent envelope work.

  **SHIPPED 2026-06-14 (PR #169 (c150572) → develop, Critic-debt refactor batch).** Pointed create_envelope's host-kind gate at the canonical TRACK_KINDS and added the planner route-completeness drift-guard test.

- **[SNP-8R4K]** Snapshot capture records the HallucinoteAnalyzer devices (+ post-render device loads land after the analyzer → per-stem captures under-measure)
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: snapshot/sync · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-13 · status: shipped · stage: design · related: BAK-3M9T, DEV-5R8Q, SDC-7K3M, MCP-4T6Y · refs: .prawduct/artifacts/plans/SNP-8R4K/design.md, .prawduct/artifacts/plans/SNP-8R4K/build-plan.md, incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-snapshot-capture-includes-hallucinote-analyzer-devices.md · reviewed: 2026-06-13 · closed-by: chunks 1-4 → develop (dacb141, aa20196, 9be32cc, 39487be; operator-verification treated complete+passed per user)`

  **CODE SHIPPED to develop 2026-06-13 — chunks 1-4 all merged (no-Live + the migration); only Live operator-verification of chunks 3+4 remains.** Chunk 1 (boundary exclusion — `analyzer_identity.is_analyzer_device` + capture/replay/pull filter + dense-renumber + push-skip; commit dacb141), chunk 2 (snapshot clean-at-rest `migrate_snapshot` + `capture_cli migrate` + version stamp + replay warning; aa20196), chunk 4 (push-preflight stale-set detection + rebuild guidance via `probe_and_link`; 9be32cc), chunk 3 (render terminal-tap reposition + `analyzer_not_terminal` observability; 39487be) — cumulative + verify-resolutions Critic clean. **REMAINING:** Live operator-verification of 3+4 (enqueued in `operator-verification.md`) — the render actually repositioning the analyzer to last + the preflight guidance firing on a real stale set; and the deferred never-tapless reposition hardening (load-new-then-delete-old). When those pass, flip status=shipped. (NOTE: a parallel agent's RTE phase-reorder is preserved in a git stash, NOT on develop — it'll re-update the phase-order tests when it lands.)

  **DESIGNED 2026-06-13 (design.md + build-plan.md).** Root frame: the analyzer is **measurement infrastructure, not authored content** — the full failure cascade (mis-tap → capture pollution → persist → push-as-own-device → **duplicate accumulation across pull/push cycles** → position drift → converger/idempotency break → cross-machine non-reproducibility → tombstone poisoning) all flows from that one defect. **Decision LOCKED (user): PERSISTENT analyzer is the durable design** (kept loaded for ad-hoc-capture simplicity; ephemeral rejected). Two mechanisms make it safe: **(1) boundary exclusion** — capture filters `is_analyzer` + dense-renumbers authored positions, push skips it (the LOAD-BEARING model-cleanliness guarantee; chunk 1, the keystone that ends the accumulation cycle + off-by-one); **(2) render-owned terminal-tap** — ensure present+strictly-last at render start (reposition only on changed surfaces) + observability flag, the durable measurement-correctness mechanism (chunk 3, Live). Plus a legacy-cleanup pass (chunk 2). Build plan staged; chunk 1 is buildable no-Live.

  **CORRECTNESS bug (swell mix dogfood, 2026-06-13) — two linked issues, same root (the analyzer is infrastructure, not authored content, but capture/load treat it as ordinary).**

  **(a) Capture pollution.** `ableton_render` leaves a `HallucinoteAnalyzer` device **permanently in every track/return/master chain** (idempotent load, never removed post-pass). `/song-snapshot` + the pull-mix/capture path iterate `ableton_device(action='list')` and there is **no analyzer filter** in `compile_snapshot` / `capture.py` (grep of `src/hallucinote/sync/pull/` and `capture.py` for `analyzer` is empty), so the refresh writes `HallucinoteAnalyzer` entries (`class_name=MxDeviceAudioEffect`, display "Max Audio Effect") into `captured_session.json`. On replay the push `devices` phase would try to **recreate** a non-loadable browser node (rebuild breaks/drifts), and because the analyzer is often **interleaved** (e.g. Hall return: reverb → analyzer → compressor), capturing it **shifts every downstream device's index** even if recreation were skipped. **Fix:** filter devices whose `name == "HallucinoteAnalyzer"` at capture (`compile_snapshot` / per-device probe loop / `capture_cli`) so they never enter the snapshot; **and** have the push `devices` phase skip a `HallucinoteAnalyzer` entry if one ever does appear (so a pre-existing polluted snapshot can't break a rebuild). Optional: `ableton_device(action='list')` carries an `is_infrastructure` flag so callers can filter generically.

  **(b) Load lands after the analyzer → per-stem under-measure.** `ableton_device(action='load')` appends to the END of the chain (Live 12.4 has no reorder API), and because the analyzer already sits last, **every device loaded after a render lands AFTER the analyzer** — so the analyzer is no longer the final node. The analyzer is a **measurement tap that must capture the chain's final output**; with devices after it, the per-stem WAV captures the signal *before* those devices, so the per-stem MixReport **silently under-measures** them (observed this session: a timpani→Celli duck, a kick→Gtr pump, a ducked Hall, a Contrabass sub-saturator were all post-analyzer → the per-stem numbers looked like "the change did nothing"; only the master capture, post-everything, reflected them). **Fix:** on `load`, insert the new device *before* a trailing `HallucinoteAnalyzer` (and/or move the analyzer back to last after the load), OR have the render's idempotent analyzer-load **reposition** the analyzer to the end of each chain (delete + re-add last) — option (b) also self-heals chains where devices were added between renders. Until fixed, the only manual fix is delete the analyzers and re-render (they reload last). Relates to **DEV-5R8Q** (device-chain ordering / no reorder API). **Verifiable signal:** a post-render `/song-snapshot` refresh contains no `HallucinoteAnalyzer` entries and preserves authored device indices; a device loaded after a render is captured (per-stem WAV) reflecting its effect (analyzer stays last); the push `devices` phase skips any analyzer entry in a polluted snapshot. (swell snapshot analyzer-pollution + load-ordering, user, 2026-06-13)

- **[SDC-7K3M]** Device sidechain SOURCE routing survives the DB round-trip — symmetric with track routing (SHIPPED author+push+pull; only operator-gated Live round-trip verification remains)
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: sync · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-13 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: RTE-1K9T, BAK-3M9T, SNP-8R4K, DPP-7H2K · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-device-sidechain-source-not-in-snapshot-model.md · reviewed: 2026-06-13 · closed-by: fix/sdc-7k3m-pull-capture (5560eb0; operator-verification treated complete+passed per user)`

  **The bug (swell mix dogfood, 2026-06-13).** Added five sidechains in Live via `ableton_device(set_sidechain)` — a machine kick-pump (kick → Bass Punk, kick → Gtr Power), a timpani-audibility duck (Timpani → Contrabass, Timpani → Celli), and a ducked reverb (PRE-MAIN → Hall return). The `S/C On`/`S/C Gain`/`S/C Mix` params round-trip as ordinary `device_parameters`, but the **SOURCE** (the device's input routing — `set_input_routing` / `set_sidechain`'s `source_display_name`) was **lost on rebuild**: every sidechained compressor came back with `S/C On = 1` but no source assigned, silently breaking the sidechain. The mix only survived in the saved `.als`, defeating the "build.py + snapshot are source-of-truth, the `.als` is regenerable" model. Full report: `incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-device-sidechain-source-not-in-snapshot-model.md`.

  **PARTIALLY SHIPPED — author + push half done.** On branch `fix/sdc-7k3m-device-sidechain-roundtrip` (commit `9868e75`, `feat(sync): SDC-7K3M — device sidechain SOURCE survives DB round-trip (author + push)`): added `devices.sidechain_source_track_id` (nullable semantic FK → tracks, survives renames like the PRE-MAIN bus FK) + `sidechain_source_channel` + migration; a `set_device_sidechain(device_id, source_track_id, channel)` mutator so build.py can author it directly; and a new push phase `device_sidechain` **after** `devices` (so the device exists first) — `plan_push_device_sidechain` emits `set_input_routing` resolving the FK to the source track's `display_name`. Symmetric with **RTE-1K9T** track routing.

  **Operator note (concurrency unblock):** a parallel agent was blocked on freeing Live until this landed — the author+push half unblocks them: author the five swell sidechains in `build.py` via `set_device_sidechain`, and push materializes them.

  **PULL-capture chunk SHIPPED (2026-06-13, branch `fix/sdc-7k3m-pull-capture`).** Added a new `device-sidechain` pull domain (`plan_pull_device_sidechain`) + `_apply_device_sidechain_source` apply handler: resolves Live `get_input_routing` `current_type` → a song-track FK by name match and writes through `set_device_sidechain`. Conservative V1 policy — a distinct-track match is captured; self / none / ambiguous / non-track-input all no-op. 13 unit tests in `tests/unit/sync/test_pull.py`. Engine-only (the MCP getter already existed; no fingerprint flip). This feeds the agent's `/snapshot-bake-recent-changes` workflow.

  **REMAINING (this item's open scope) — only operator-gated Live round-trip verification:** Live was occupied at author/pull-build time, so the end-to-end check is enqueued in `operator-verification.md`. **Verifiable signal:** a sidechain set in Live is captured by pull into `devices.sidechain_source_track_id`, survives a full `--reset` rebuild + push, and the source re-resolves to the correct track. Per repo convention this item is **shipped-pending-operator-verification** — kept open at this reduced scope until the Live round-trip passes.

- **[SNP-4K7M]** Master-track devices aren't snapshot-authorable / no capture round-trip — push side exists, authorship middle missing
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: snapshot · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-12 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: DEV-6M2K, TPL-2D8K, BAK-3M9T · reviewed: 2026-06-13 · closed-by: PR #166 → develop`

  The PRE-MAIN submaster bus (v0.9.5) makes a master ceiling limiter a real need (the bus "swell" rides post-chain, so only a master-track post-fader device catches it), but a master device can't be authored as song source-of-truth. Full report: `incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-12-master-devices-not-in-snapshot-authorship-model.md`.

  **The asymmetry — only the authorship middle is missing.** **Push side: present** — DEV-6M2K (v0.9.5, live-verified) loads master-chain devices and the render auto-loads the analyzer onto the master. **Snapshot + capture: ABSENT** — the snapshot models master as `song.master = {volume, panning}` with **no `devices` array** (`src/hallucinote/capture.py:24-45`); `replay_capture` (`:371-397`) creates no master devices (contrast tracks at `:509-524`, which check `if t.get("devices")`); and `capture_plan` (`:537-589`) never probes the master device chain, so `/song-snapshot` can't round-trip a hand-placed master Limiter. Net: the push can build a master device, but nothing the *song author* writes (snapshot, `/song-pick-instruments`, `/song-snapshot`) can declare or preserve one — violating "sound design is authorship" exactly at the master.

  **Fix direction (from the report):** (1) add `song.master.devices` mirroring the per-track/return device encoding + have `replay_capture` materialize the master chain; (2) extend `capture.py`/`/song-snapshot` to probe the master chain (closes the loop with #1); (3) document the `create_device_chain(parent_track_id=master_id)` mutator path *if* it already works for a `kind='master'` track (UNVERIFIED in the report — the master currently has zero `device_chains` rows). Plus a one-line pointer in `docs/song-authoring-conventions.md` "PRE-MAIN submaster bus": put the safety ceiling on the master (post-fader), and note master devices aren't yet snapshot-authorable.

  **Verifiable signal:** a `build.py` snapshot declares a master Limiter, a from-scratch push materializes it on the master, and `/song-snapshot` round-trips a hand-placed master device back into the snapshot. **Sized:** medium (snapshot schema + replay + capture probe + a decision-record). (swell PRE-MAIN dogfood, 2026-06-12)

- **[MIX-3S7P]** Per-section pan + reverb-send automation (atmospheric space that snaps back) — host-clip strategy DECIDED + shipped + verified-pending-render
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: mix · source: user · added: 2026-06-01 · status: shipped · related: AUD-8H2M, ENV-3M7K, MIX-7K2D, ENV-9P4T · stage: ready · refs: .prawduct/artifacts/plans/MIX-3S7P/design.md · reviewed: 2026-06-13 · closed-by: chunks 1+3 shipped; chunk 2 operator-verification treated passed per user`

  **Raised by the user (2026-06-01, sun-zone-done back-half rework):** *"Let's play with pan and reverb a bit more, especially in intro and break. Nothing cheesy or annoying, just… a little more space. Then snap back to the baseline when we get to verse/chorus."* The intent: the atmospheric sections (intro = the dawn polyrhythm cloud; break = the ethereal eureka suspension) get a wider image + a wetter Plate tail, returning to the baseline mix at verse1 and integration.

  **Why deferred (not a quick change).** `mixer_pan` + `send_level` envelopes are **session-clip routed** (Live 12.4 LOM — confirmed in `src/hallucinote/sync/pull/envelopes.py:237,406` and the `songs/sun-zone-done/sun-zone-done.md` "Deferred" note): each needs a hosting clip covering its beat range, exactly the constraint the Amp Type envelope hit (solved there by ONE monolithic 736-beat Rhythm Gtr clip). And the exact amounts ("a little more space") are render-gated to tune by ear. So it's a host-clip-strategy decision + automation authoring + a render pass, not a one-line tweak.

  **The ask.** Decide the host-clip strategy for per-section MIXER/SEND automation (the mixer analog of the Amp-envelope monolithic-clip pattern): either (a) give the automated tracks a monolithic clip to host their mixer envelopes, or (b) author per-section session-clip-routed envelope segments, or (c) a cleaner mechanism. Then author the first use: intro + break get an elevated Plate send + a slightly wider pan on organ/lead/steel, snapping to baseline at verse1 + integration. Couples to AUD-8H2M (the analyzer can't yet VERIFY time-varying mix moves landed in the render — same envelope-realization blind spot). Kept STATIC for v1 (conservative sends in `captured_session.json`).

  **Verifiable signal:** a decision-record naming the host-clip strategy for mixer/send envelopes + sun-zone-done authoring per-section pan/send automation for intro+break that returns to baseline (verifiable by the breakpoints in the DB; realization render-gated). **Sized:** medium. (user pan/reverb-space request, 2026-06-01)

This item became a VERIFY+RECORD pass, not a build:

Decision-record (chosen strategy, alternatives (a) monolith / (c) auto-partition, the two-coexisting-host-patterns contrast, trade-offs) lives in `.prawduct/artifacts/plans/MIX-3S7P/design.md` §2, linking the song-local record (LINK-DON'T-SUMMARIZE).

The three by-ear amounts (pan width, the "nothing cheesy" Plate-send wetness, the boundary-snap feel) are surfaced as PENDING-by-ear in `decisions/08` "MIX-3S7P verification" — NOT guessed (Honest Confidence).
  - **PENDING USER DECISION (NOT a chunk, do not fork):** the **song-spanning dynamic DubDelay send** (dry-reggae / wet-metal across the whole arc) is the ONE case the clip-local trick does not solve — it crosses the metal-chorus gaps where the source track is tacet. The user directed v1 to keep the **static Lead DubDelay send** ("stays a static send for v1"). Carried as a creative fork the user owns; do not build without a re-lock. The candidate general mechanism is **ENV-3M7K** (planner auto-partition — split a song-spanning envelope into per-host-clip sub-envelopes; the planner already names this as its own v1.1 follow-up at `src/hallucinote/sync/push/envelopes.py:469`), or a monolithic host clip on a never-tacet track. (MIX-3S7P VERIFY+RECORD, 2026-06-03)

  **Update (2026-06-11): the song-spanning send is now MECHANICALLY routable via perform (ENV-9P4T chunk 02).** The ONE case the clip-local trick couldn't solve — a send envelope that must persist across the metal-chorus gaps where the source track is tacet — is now plan-routable: with plain/audio-track perform routing (ENV-9P4T chunk 02, commit `cb54ce2`), an uncovered send envelope routes to a *continuous arrangement lane* instead of warn-and-skipping at the clip gap. So the mechanism the deferred dry→wet DubDelay arc was waiting on EXISTS (this generalizes / partly subsumes the ENV-3M7K auto-partition route for this case). **NOT done here:** the actual sun-zone-done DubDelay authoring + the by-ear creative re-lock (the dry-reggae / wet-metal amounts across the arc) stay **user-owned** — a creative fork, not a chunk; do not build without the user's re-lock. - **[ARR-9K4T]** Recurrence/form has no read-side — motif recall & recapitulation are authored but unverifiable — measurement-coverage gap
  `effort: M · impact: L · area: recurrence · source: user · added: 2026-06-01 · status: shipped · related: ARR-8P5K, MEL-1A7K · stage: design · reviewed: 2026-06-13 · closed-by: recurrence read-side shipped (recurrence_lens.py)`

  **Both-sides gap.** RECURRENCE/FORM is a first-class authored dimension — `Arrangement.motif()` + `vary()` + reference — and recapitulation is load-bearing in sun-zone-done (the integration QUOTES the registered `polyrhythm-cloud` motif; the outro AUGMENTS the `no-time-stab` motif via `V.augment`). The AUTHORING side is shipped; the READ side does not exist. **No tool verifies a registered motif was actually recalled, detects a recapitulation, or measures motivic economy** (is the song built from a small recurring cell-set, or scattered?). The melody lens's motivic/n-gram reading is explicitly NOT-YET (`melody/lens.py:48-50`) and is line-level anyway; this gap is the *cross-instrument / arrangement-level* recurrence read (e.g. "the integration organ's notes ARE `shift`s of the polyrhythm motif — confirmed"). MEL-1A7K owns the melodic-LINE motivic-economy slice; this is the structural recurrence-realization sibling under ARR-8P5K.

  **Verifiable signal:** a read-side that, given an arrangement, reports which registered motifs recur where (and as which variation: transpose/augment/invert/…) plus a motivic-economy summary, wired into `/compose-review`; OR a decision-record states recurrence-realization stays composer-owned with rationale. Today: no motif-recall / recapitulation reader exists in `src/hallucinote/` analysis or lens code (the few incidental `recur` substring hits are unrelated).

- **[AUD-3T6L]** Reverb RT60 tolerance (±0.15 s) is too tight for in-mix intent-matching
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: audio · source: verification · added: 2026-06-02 · status: shipped · stage: ready · reviewed: 2026-06-13 · closed-by: PR #166 → develop`

  Reverb verification compares the measured decay-tail RT60 against the composer's declared INTENT (Plate 3.0 s, Room 0.8 s) with a fixed ±0.15 s band (`REVERB_TOLERANCE_S`). With the ring-out anchor fixed, sun-zone-done measured A-Plate **3.37 s vs 3.0** and B-Room **1.26 s vs 0.8** — both flagged out-of-tolerance. But (a) Live's Reverb RT60 is a nonlinear function of Decay Time + Room Size + diffusion, so the realized RT60 legitimately diverges from the nominal device knob, and (b) in-mix measurement at low wet SNR carries real error. A ±0.15 s ABSOLUTE band is unrealistically tight. Consider a relative tolerance (~±15–20 %), an SNR/span-aware confidence band, or reframing as a "decays N % longer/shorter than intent" producer's note rather than a binary verdict. **Verifiable signal:** a sub-0.5 s realized-vs-intent gap on a clean (high-span) capture no longer reads as a hard warning. **Sized:** small. (reverb verification session 2026-06-02)

  **Moved Archive → Open (2026-06-09 triage):** `pending` is not an archive status; this is open, un-started work.

  `effort: S · impact: S · area: device · source: critic · added: 2026-05-18 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: D4 (305742c) · reviewed: 2026-06-13`

The item's own text anticipated this ("the round-trip may be structurally solved"). No remaining work in this repo.

Load works for either source class_name; but when the device is captured (`device.list`), the reported `class_name` may be the merged form, breaking deterministic re-push. Post-D4 (commit `305742c`, "structural display-name shift — delete `_CLASS_TO_DISPLAY`"), the mapping was restructured to `device_names.py` with rack-root lookup only — the round-trip may be structurally solved. Real-Live verify path: load via `kind='Flanger'`, re-capture, confirm class_name preserved. If not, planner needs `preset_uri` for merged-display devices. **Verifiable signal:** real-Live smoke confirms class_name preservation; or a unit test pins the post-D4 invariant. (critic W2 N1, Chunk W2-B 2026-05-18; D4 context added 2026-05-22)

- **[RTE-2P9X]** Push `routing` phase precedes `devices` — a freshly-created MIDI track can't route its (audio) output to a bus
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: sync · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-12 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: 7fd926e · related: RTE-1K9T, PSH-2R7K · reviewed: 2026-06-13`

  **SHIPPED — RELIABILITY-CLUSTER, merged to develop in `7fd926e` (Merge RELIABILITY-CLUSTER), 2026-06-13.** Phase-ordering fix so a freshly-created MIDI track's audio-bus output routing resolves after the instrument loads.

  Full report: `incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-12-routing-phase-precedes-devices-new-midi-track.md`. Surfaced by the first adoption of the 0.9.5 PRE-MAIN bus in `swell`.

  **The bug.** Phase order is `… mix → routing → devices …` (`src/hallucinote/sync/push/plan.py:54-68` — routing idx 7, devices idx 8). A newly-created MIDI track has **no instrument** until the devices phase; with no instrument it emits MIDI, so its output-routing surface is MIDI-style (other tracks + `No Output`) — an **audio** bus isn't a valid target yet. So `routing` hard-fails for that track (`set_output_routing: ValueError: 'PRE-MAIN' not in available [...]`) and the push halts at `routing`. Bit exactly one track in `swell` (the brand-new `22 Meter Hat`); the 21 pre-existing instrument tracks already had audio output, so `PRE-MAIN` resolved for them. Not a naming/resolution problem — an instrument-less MIDI track has no audio output to route, and `routing.py` has no instrument-awareness/deferral.

  **Fix direction (from the report, preference order):** (1) route MIDI-track audio outputs in a sub-pass **after** `devices` (keep input routing / monitor state / audio-track→bus routing where they are); (2) make the routing planner instrument-aware — defer a MIDI track's audio-bus output call to after devices when no instrument exists yet; (3) at minimum, a teaching halt for this exact case (new MIDI track, audio-bus target, no instrument) instead of the generic "not in available" list. Plus a one-line caveat in `docs/song-authoring-conventions.md` "PRE-MAIN submaster bus".

  **Verifiable signal:** a first push that *creates* a new MIDI track and routes its output to an audio bus completes all phases (the audio-output routing resolves after the instrument loads), with no manual instrument pre-load workaround. **Sized:** medium (a post-devices routing sub-pass or instrument-aware deferral in the planner). (swell PRE-MAIN dogfood, 2026-06-12)

  **RE-CONFIRMED with a decisive empirical test (swell-push dogfood, 2026-06-13 — `2026-06-13-push-routing-audio-bus-not-available-fresh-set.md`).** Reproduced twice on swell into a fresh set; halts at `routing`, never reaching `devices`/`device_sidechain`/`performed_automation`. **Root cause confirmed by before/after, not hypothesised:** on the fresh set with `devices` not yet run, `ableton_device(list, track=6)` = `[]` and `set_output_routing(track=6, 'PRE-MAIN')` FAILS (available = MIDI tracks + `No Output` only, no audio tracks); load the instrument and retry the SAME call → SUCCEEDS. That single flip with nothing else changed proves it's the instrument (hence phase order), not Monitor state, not the default scaffold. **An earlier draft's "PRE-MAIN needs Monitor=In first" hypothesis is REFUTED** by this test (Monitor=In never even got set — the phase halts before configuring the bus; that's a symptom, not the cause). Fix preference unchanged: reorder so `devices` precedes `routing` (as shipped: `tracks → returns → scenes → clips → mix → devices → routing → device_sidechain → …` — see `_PHASE_NAMES`, plan.py:54-67); add a fresh-set first-push regression test for the PRE-MAIN pattern (existing coverage clearly ran on already-instrumented tracks). **PSH-2R7K (`execute --only devices` / `--start-at routing`) is the operational mitigation** for this class of phase-ordering surprise. (swell fresh-set dogfood, 2026-06-13)

- **[SYN-3C8K]** `probe-and-link` leaves stale **clip** links on a Live-set swap → clips phase halts on re-push
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: sync · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-12 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: 7fd926e · reviewed: 2026-06-13`

  **SHIPPED — RELIABILITY-CLUSTER, merged to develop in `7fd926e` (Merge RELIABILITY-CLUSTER), 2026-06-13.** Reconciliation now cascades stale clip-link drops on a Live-set swap so the clips phase no longer halts on re-push.

  Full report + repro: `incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-12-probe-and-link-stale-clip-links.md`.

  **The bug.** `probe-and-link`'s strict reconciliation enumerates the probe's **tracks and returns** and drops links whose `ableton_index` is absent, but **never reconciles `clip` links** (`src/hallucinote/sync/push/probe.py:477-513` iterates only track/return kinds; the `:479` comment assumes nested kinds cascade from parent deletion, which doesn't fire when a stale clip link persists). When a session is reused across a Live-set swap, the stale clip links survive; the clips planner keys create-vs-replace off link presence, so it emits `replace_notes`-only against empty slots → `IndexError: session slot N on track M is empty` on **every** clip → push **halts at the clips phase**. Narrow trigger (only session-reuse-across-set-swap; a fresh `--auto-session` push never hits it), but it halts the whole push.

  **Fix direction (from the report, cleanest first):** (1) cascade clip-link drops in reconciliation — when a track link is dropped (or a clip's resolved `(track, slot)` is empty in the probe), drop its `clip` links too; (2) make the clips planner verify slot non-emptiness against the in-hand `--probe` snapshot before downgrading to replace-only.

  **Secondary (same code path):** `default_scaffold_unmatched_tracks` comes back **empty on session reuse** because the clean-default-scaffold classifier is gated on `auto_session_created == true` (`probe.py:536`), so the "delete the default scaffold? (Y/n)" UX degrades to the generic Case-2 confirm on reuse. Classify the scaffold regardless of whether the session was freshly minted.

  **Verifiable signal:** reusing a session across a Live-set swap, re-push reconciliation drops the stale clip links (or the planner re-`create`s the empty slots) so the clips phase completes; and `default_scaffold_unmatched_tracks` is populated on reuse. **Sized:** medium (reconciliation cascade + the classifier gate). (swell set-swap dogfood, 2026-06-12)

- **[DPP-7H2K]** Device-param `value_display` addressability + sub-display precision echo + sidechain/routing doc fix (bare track name)
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: mcp-bridge/device · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-13 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: 7fd926e · related: SDC-7K3M, SYN-9F2L · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-13-device-param-value-display-precision-and-docs.md · reviewed: 2026-06-13`

  **SHIPPED — RELIABILITY-CLUSTER, merged to develop in `7fd926e` (Merge RELIABILITY-CLUSTER), 2026-06-13.** `value_display` explicit-unit addressability, sub-display precision echo, and the bare-track-name sidechain/routing doc fix.

  **Three friction points from the swell mix dogfood (2026-06-13), heavy `set_parameter` + `set_sidechain` use — each minor alone but each cost extra round-trips.**

  **(a) `value_display` refuses non-monotonic params, leaving only raw-curve guesswork.** Setting a compressor Release or a sidechain `S/C EQ Freq` by display value fails with `DisplayValueError: …display isn't monotonic across its range ('1.00 ms'..'3.00 s') — its leading number reverses (ms→s)… use the normalized value`. The refusal is *correct* (the leading digit isn't monotonic across the unit change) but **dumps the caller onto the raw [0,1] curve with no real-unit path** — the agent had to reverse-engineer the frequency curve from two probed points (`(0.158→80 Hz),(0.305→200 Hz) ⇒ norm = (log10 f − log10 30)/(log10 15000 − log10 30)`) to set 150 Hz as 0.259. Every agent hits this for EQ freqs, comp release/attack, LFO rates. **Fix:** parse an explicit-unit display string and key off the unit rather than the leading-digit heuristic — `value_display='120 ms'`, `'2.5 s'`, `'150 Hz'`, `'2 kHz'` are each unambiguous; the monotonicity guard only needs to fall back to "raw required" when the *parsed, unit-normalized* target is still ambiguous (rare). Alternatives: expose typed setters (`value_hz` / `value_ms` / `value_db`), or return the param's (raw→display) sample points in `get_parameters` so the caller inverts locally without probing.

  **(b) Display rounding (0.01 Hz) too coarse for phase-critical params.** The Phaser-Flanger `Mod Freq` displays in 0.01 Hz steps; aligning the phaser to a 136-beat span (10 cycles ⇒ 0.1544 Hz) needed ~3% precision, but `value_display='0.1544 Hz'` inverted to a raw that read back as "0.16 Hz" (ambiguous overshoot) and the readback can't distinguish 0.150 from 0.1544 (both round to "0.15") — so neither setting NOR verifying a phase-critical rate is possible through the display surface (the agent set the raw 0.32762 from a 2-point fit and accepted being unable to confirm). **Fix:** in the `set_parameter` response, echo the achieved **raw** value (already present) AND a higher-precision computed **real-unit** value (not the device's rounded display string); bonus: a `get_parameters` option returning display at full float precision.

  **(c) DOC fix (fast doc-only win — consider splitting out).** The `set_sidechain` / `set_input_routing` help example shows the index-prefixed form `source_display_name='1-Drums'`, but Live 12.x accepts the **BARE track name** — `'2-02 Kit Punk'` fails (`input routing type … not in available [...]`), `'02 Kit Punk'` works; returns appear as `A-Hall`, master as `Main`, plus `No Input`/`No Output`. The `<index>-<name>` example sends agents down a wrong first attempt every time. **Fix:** update the examples/param docs to the bare track/return-name form (note the return/master/No-Input vocabulary), or have the resolver also accept the index-prefixed form. **This VALIDATES the just-shipped SDC-7K3M push** (which correctly emits the bare track name) and is a quick doc fix — split (c) out as a fast win if convenient. **Verifiable signal:** a non-monotonic param (EQ freq, comp release) is settable by an explicit-unit display string (`'150 Hz'`/`'120 ms'`) without raw-curve reverse-engineering; `set_parameter` echoes a sub-display-precision real-unit value so a phase-critical rate is verifiable; the `set_sidechain`/`set_input_routing` docs show the bare track-name form. (swell device-param display addressability + doc fix, user, 2026-06-13)

- **[INV-3K8W]** preset_query teaching errors point away from the fix (pattern-with-`/` + path_prefix[0]==root)
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: inventory · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-10 · status: shipped · stage: ready · closed-by: 7d2dcde · refs: incoming-bugs/archives/2026-06-10-swell-first-compose-friction.md · reviewed: 2026-06-13`

  **SHIPPED in CLR-A (#160, merged `7d2dcde`) — backlog-drift catch (a BLG-7K2Q-style drift catch: shipped in CLR-A, never closed on the backlog).** Verified this session: the teaching-error fix (code + tests) is present in #160; the item had been left `open`, closed here as `shipped`.

  From the swell first-compose friction log (item 1). When a `preset_query` pattern contains a `/`, the error should teach name-only matching + `path_prefix` (the actual fix), not point elsewhere; and `path_prefix[0]==root` should be detected and taught explicitly. Today the teaching errors send the agent away from the correct call shape. **Verifiable signal:** a pattern-with-`/` query and a root-leading `path_prefix` each return an error that names the correct call shape (name-only pattern + path_prefix; drop the root segment).

- **[SYN-6B4Q]** Skeleton push goes PARTIAL at cues phase — cues planner should skip-with-warning beyond current arrangement extent
