- **[GEN-5K2D]** Wave 0 paper-cut: polyrhythm helper using `fractions.Fraction`
  `effort: S · impact: S · area: generators · source: builder · added: 2026-05-19 · status: shipped · closed-by: fix/backlog-low-cost-sweep · reviewed: 2026-06-09`

  `(7.0 / 5) * 3 / 2.0` yields `2.0999999999999996` (IEEE 754 sub-LSB drift). The SQLite REAL column round-trips it faithfully, but authoring introduces it without warning. A future `hallucinote.polyrhythm(n, against=k)` helper should compute via `fractions.Fraction(against, n)` and float-convert only at the mutator boundary. (Wave 0 canary `odd-meter-experimental` runbook step 8, 2026-05-19)

- **[ARR-7M3D]** Energy-realization is unmeasured in audio (declared curve vs rendered intensity) — measurement-coverage gap
  `effort: M · impact: L · area: energy · source: user · added: 2026-06-01 · status: shipped · closed-by: feature/arr-7m3d-energy-realization · related: ARR-8P5K, ARR-2S9D · reviewed: 2026-06-09`

  **Signal MET (2026-06-03, `feature/arr-7m3d-energy-realization`):** the read-side
  check exists and is wired in. `sections.energy` is persisted (the source-of-truth
  fix the curve never reaching the DB); `MixReport.energy_realization`
  (`src/hallucinote/audio/energy.py::realize_energy`) joins the declared
  `[SectionEnergy]` to measured per-section intensity BY `start_beat` and reports
  per-correlate Spearman ρ (loudness + onset density) + rank inversions, with the
  B1 nan contract (tied/constant → ρ `None`, never nan; `allow_nan=False` write
  backstop) and the W2 measured-nan symmetry. Wired into `/mix-review` MEASURE +
  INTERPRET (DR-4) and pointed at from `/compose-review` + `arrangement-model.md`.
  Ruler-not-stamp lock test present. PENDING attended-run (Live unattended this
  run, flagged in `.prawduct/operator-verification.md`): the e2e objective ρ read
  on a real sun-zone-done render + the by-ear DR-5 surfacing-threshold tune
  (currently the conservative surface-everything default). Spectral correlate
  deferred → ARR-2S9D below (DR-3, flagged-not-dropped).

  **Both-sides gap (ARR-8P5K's own principle: "a dimension authored but unmeasured is half-built").** ENERGY is a first-class authored dimension — `Arrangement.section(..., energy=)` → `arr.energy_curve` (sun-zone-done declares 0.25→1.0 across 9 sections). The READ side is only *symbolic*: `/compose-review` reads whether the authored curve "builds, breathes, peaks" from build.py/arrangement (SKILL.md L61) — it never checks the audio. The MixReport measures per-section loudness (`audio/analyze.py` `_measure_sections` → `SectionMetrics`) but **nothing joins the two**: no tool confirms the declared energy[section] is actually realized as rendered intensity (loudness + spectral density + onset rate). A section authored energy=0.9 that renders quieter/sparser than an energy=0.6 section ships unflagged — the exact "is the chorus actually lifting?" question, gone dark on the audio side. Harmony (ARR-1H9C conformance lint) and performance (perf lens) both got the realization check; energy did not.

  **Verifiable signal:** a read-side check exists that takes the arrangement's `energy_curve` + a MixReport and reports per-section declared-vs-measured intensity divergence (rank-correlation of declared energy against measured loudness/density, flagging inversions), wired into `/mix-review`; OR a decision-record states energy-realization stays a by-ear judgment with rationale. Today: no reference to the authored `energy_curve` anywhere in `src/hallucinote/audio/` — the curve never reaches the audio analyzer (the incidental `energy` hits there are all acoustic/spectral energy, a different quantity).

- **[SYN-4P2D]** First push of a >8-section song into a fresh default Live set hard-fails — set ships with only 8 scenes, push doesn't auto-create them, raw per-clip IndexError
  `effort: S · impact: L · area: sync · source: user · added: 2026-06-01 · status: shipped · closed-by: #136 (fix/syn-4p2d-scenes-provisioning) · reviewed: 2026-06-09`

  **Recurring first-push trap (user, 2026-06-01: "a real gap that will bite us over and
  over").** A default Ableton Live set ALWAYS ships with exactly 8 scenes, so the FIRST
  push (auto-session, fresh set) of ANY song with >8 sections fails *deterministically*
  at the `clips` phase — this is the common new-song path, not an edge case.
  Hit live pushing sun-zone-done (9 sections) into a fresh default Live set (8 scenes,
  2026-06-01). The `clips` phase creates one session clip per section in scene slots
  1..N; if the set has fewer than N scenes, every section-N clip fails with
  `ableton_clip('create') failed: IndexError: clip_index 9 out of range [1, 8]` (one per
  affected track — here 5), halting `execute` at `clips` (4/10 phases). The push does
  NOT create the scenes it needs, and the failure surfaces as N raw per-clip IndexErrors
  rather than one actionable message. Workaround that unblocked it:
  `ableton_scene(action='create')` to add the 9th scene, then re-run execute (idempotent).
  Fix options (pick one): (a) the planner emits a `scenes` phase ensuring
  `scene_count >= max section slot` before `clips`; (b) `clips` auto-creates a missing
  slot on demand; (c) at minimum a pre-flight coherence check that fails fast with "song
  needs N scenes; set has M — add N−M" instead of per-clip IndexErrors. **Verifiable
  signal:** push a ≥9-section song into a default 8-scene set and it completes (or fails
  with the single actionable message), not 5 raw IndexErrors.

  **Dedup note (2026-06-03):** absorbs the PSH-1S9C dogfood duplicate (same bug — a >8-section song pushed into a fresh default 8-scene set hard-fails at the `clips` phase because the push doesn't auto-provision scenes). PSH-1S9C dropped with `closes: SYN-4P2D`.

  **SHIPPED on develop (#136, commit eff5398, merge `fix/syn-4p2d-scenes-provisioning`):** the push now auto-provisions scenes before the `clips` phase. Closed + archived in the 2026-06-09 triage (the close was missed at merge time — close-in-the-same-PR discipline).

- **[MCP-2K9F]** `probe set` reports a silently-ignored LOM write as `ok:true` — add a `changed`/`applied` read-back comparison
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: mcp · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-18 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: MCP-7P3R · closed-by: PR#181 (238538d), PR#183 · refs: incoming-bugs/2026-06-18-no-mcp-path-from-session-override-back-to-arrangement.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  Correctness bug (severity M), GENERAL (not arrangement-specific — affects EVERY `ableton_probe(action='set')` call). `set_handler` (`hallucinote_mcp/handlers/probe.py:219-245`) reads `old`, does a bare `setattr(parent, attr, _resolve_arg(context, value))`, reads back `new`, and returns both **with no comparison**. When Live silently ignores the write (no exception thrown — a known LOM quirk for certain props), the result is `ok:true` with `old==new` — **indistinguishable from a legitimate set-to-the-same-value**. An agent reaching for `probe set` on such a prop gets a false "ok" and silently-wrong behavior. **Surfaced** dogfooding swell while recovering a session-override (`setattr(song,'back_to_arranger',False)` returned `old==true,new==true`; `setattr(song,'current_song_time',968)` returned `old==new`, no move — while `ableton_session(action='seek')` relocated the playhead correctly). **Fix:** add `changed: old != new` to the result, and for a settable scalar whose read-back ≠ the requested value, flag it (`applied: false` + a teaching warning) instead of a bare `ok:true` — turning "settability is a probe finding" (per the docstring) into an *observable* one. **Teaching copy the maintainer can lift** (report §"these surfaces should teach"): "Write did not land: read-back (`true`) ≠ requested (`false`). Live silently ignored this `setattr`. `song.back_to_arranger` cannot be cleared via the API — use Live's **Back to Arrangement** button. `song.current_song_time` won't move via `set` — use `ableton_session(action='seek')`." **Verifiable signal:** a `probe set` whose read-back ≠ the requested value returns `applied:false`/a warning (not bare `ok:true`), and `changed` is present on every `set` result. **Server-side** (`handlers/probe.py`, in the MCP `_FINGERPRINT_PATHS`) ⇒ flips the fingerprint, Live-gated to verify the silent-ignore cases. (session-override recovery dogfood, swell, 2026-06-18)

  **SHIPPED (2026-06-18):** core read-back comparison shipped via **PR #181** (commit `238538d` on develop) — `probe set` now returns `changed` on every result and flags `applied:false` + a teaching warning when read-back ≠ requested. **Refined in PR #183:** float-repr tolerance added via `_request_differs` so a legitimate set whose read-back differs only in float representation isn't falsely flagged as silently-ignored.

- **[MCP-8H4N]** Version-mismatch error hint misdiagnoses a stale *server process* as a stale Remote Script
  `effort: S · impact: M · area: mcp · source: user · added: 2026-06-17 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: MCP-7F2K · closed-by: PR#181 (238538d), PR#183 · refs: incoming-bugs/2026-06-14-version-mismatch-hint-misdiagnoses-stale-server-process.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  The `hallucinote_mcp` version-gate error (raised on every tool call when server-half and Remote-Script-half fingerprints differ) hard-codes a one-sided hint: *"If the Remote Script side is stale (the common case): run `/ableton-mcp-install` … then restart Live."* For the dev-repo-as-plugin setup (`--plugins-dir ~/source/hallucinote`), that is frequently the **wrong fix**: after `git pull` + re-vendor + reopen Live + reconnect the TCP bridge, the uv-launched MCP **server process** is left holding pre-pull code in memory while both on-disk halves already agree. Observed: server reports `9906f6`, Remote Script `e10695`; `preflight` → on-disk source = `e10695` = vendored Remote Script (`matches_mcp_server: true`); `ableton://server/info` → running server = `9906f6` from the **same** source dir that on-disk fingerprints as `e10695`. Same directory, two fingerprints ⇒ files edited after the server started ⇒ the stale half is the **running server process**, not the Remote Script. Re-vendoring re-emits `e10695` (which Live already has) and a Live restart does nothing for a stale server — the real fix is to respawn the MCP server (`/mcp` reconnect, or restart Claude Code). **This is distinct from MCP-7F2K** (which relocates server-side-only handlers out of the fingerprint paths so they stop flipping the fingerprint) — this item is the **3-way-diagnosis hint fix**: make the version-gate error *diagnose before prescribing* using the three facts it already has cheaply. **Suggested fix:** (1) compare the running-server fingerprint against the *on-disk package* fingerprint (not just the Remote Script); if they differ but on-disk package == Remote Script, the **server process is stale** → tell the user to restart the MCP server, not re-vendor; (2) only when Remote Script != on-disk package should the hint recommend `/ableton-mcp-install` + Live restart; (3) bonus — have `/ableton-push` Step 0a run this three-way comparison and print the correct remediation so the orchestrator doesn't hand-derive it from `preflight` + `ableton://server/info`. Low-frequency, high-confusion (a user following the literal hint re-vendors + restarts Live repeatedly while the real culprit never changes). (dev-repo dogfood, user, 2026-06-14)

  **SHIPPED (2026-06-18):** the version-mismatch hint now diagnoses a stale server process via **PR #181** (commit `238538d` on develop). **Wording corrected in PR #183:** removed an unverifiable claim from the hint and reworded it to handle the compound stale-state case (both halves potentially stale) without over-prescribing a single fix.

- **[MCP-7F2K]** MCP version fingerprint over-triggers re-vendor — hash the wire CONTRACT, not handler implementation bodies
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: mcp · source: user · added: 2026-06-16 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: INS-3W8P, INS-4H8M, MCP-9R3T, MCP-5N8K, MCP-8H4N · closed-by: PR#183 (f7164cb) · refs: incoming-bugs/2026-06-14-version-mismatch-hint-misdiagnoses-stale-server-process.md, .prawduct/artifacts/mcp-fingerprint-design.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  Today `hallucinote_mcp.__version__` = `BASE_VERSION` + content-hash of `_FINGERPRINT_PATHS`, which includes the whole `handlers/` and `actions/` directories. Any edit to any handler body flips the fingerprint, so the server↔Remote-Script version handshake reports drift and prompts a full `/ableton-mcp-install` re-vendor — even for **server-internal changes that never cross the wire** (concrete trigger: the 2026-06-16 captures-dir fix in `handlers/analysis.py`, a pure read-side analysis change, flips it). Goal: **ONLY genuine wire-contract-breaking changes should force a re-vendor.**

  **Candidate directions to evaluate in discovery (do not pre-pick):** (a) narrow `_FINGERPRINT_PATHS` to truly wire-crossing files only — risks false-negatives when a handler change DOES alter a response shape; (b) fingerprint the DECLARED contract structurally — derive a signature from the action/tool registry + schema (tool names, action names, param names/types, response field names) so it changes iff the interface changes, decoupled from implementation bytes; (c) separate the Remote-Script-vendored surface (`remote_script/`) from server-side-only handlers that aren't vendored into Live at all, and only re-vendor on changes to the former.

  This is a **correctness-vs-friction tradeoff** (false re-vendor prompts erode trust in the handshake) and the fingerprint format is **lock-in**, so it warrants discovery before code. Type: bugfix/debt. **Stage: research** — route to `/prawduct:discovery` to evaluate the three directions and settle the fingerprint mechanism before implementation. (user, 2026-06-16)

  **Design decided (2026-06-17) → stage: ready.** Design decided in `.prawduct/artifacts/mcp-fingerprint-design.md` — root cause is server-side-only handlers (`handlers/analysis.py`, `runs_server_side=True`, never executes in Live) being hashed into the fingerprint. Decision: approach (c)→(a) — structurally relocate `runs_server_side` handlers out of `_FINGERPRINT_PATHS` keyed off the declared `runs_server_side` flag, enforced by an isolation grep-test; reject pure contract-hash (b) as primary (it would suppress re-vendor for Live-side handler bug fixes); keep hard/soft version split (b as additive) as optional phase-2. Preserves INS-3W8P. Ready to build.

  **BUILT (fix/mcp-reliability-papercuts, commit `e9f6dba`, 2026-06-18).** Relocated the server-side surface into a new **top-level** `hallucinote_mcp.server_side` package (`handlers/analysis.py` → `server_side/analysis.py`, `actions/analysis.py` → `server_side/analysis_actions.py`); it is excluded from `_FINGERPRINT_PATHS` by simply not being a listed dir (zero new walk logic — the `node_features`/`resources` pattern). Relocating the **action spec too** closes the design's `actions/`-granularity open question (analysis param-schema changes also stop flipping the fingerprint). `actions/__init__.py` imports `server_side` as the registration trigger (preserves the "import actions → all registered" invariant); `isolated_actions` pops `server_side` too. `server_side` is still vendored + imported on Live (registration cascade, as `actions/analysis.py` was) so no Live-side regression — `verify_remote_script` (source-derived) expects it, and the import-safety load-chain scan now covers `server_side/`. New `test_server_side_isolation.py` locks: runs_server_side ⇔ server_side handler, no Live-side import of server_side, and the fingerprint walk hashes no server_side file. **Live-gated remainder:** one-time re-vendor to confirm the handshake end-to-end after this ships (the commit itself flips the fingerprint once, by design). Phase-2 (b) hard/soft contract-hash split still deferred.

  **SHIPPED (2026-06-18):** merged via **PR #183** (squash commit `f7164cb` on develop). The server-side-handler relocation out of the fingerprint paths is live. **Remainder is Live-gated** and split into follow-up **MCP-4B7W** (phase-2(b) hard/soft contract-hash split + the one-time re-vendor handshake verify).

- **[MCP-7P3R]** No MCP path back to Arrangement after a Session clip overrides a track — recovery action + locate-and-play + preview-workflow guard + in-band teaching
  `effort: M · impact: M · area: mcp · source: dogfood · added: 2026-06-18 · status: shipped · stage: ready · related: MCP-2K9F, ARR-6T8N, PSH-3H8M, ING-2S7K, ING-9H2T · closed-by: PR#183 (f7164cb) · refs: incoming-bugs/2026-06-18-no-mcp-path-from-session-override-back-to-arrangement.md · reviewed: 2026-06-18`

  Authoring recovery dead-end (severity M), an MCP arrangement-playback-affordance cluster. Once a Session clip fires on a track whose real content lives in the Arrangement, there is **no working MCP affordance to return that track to Arrangement playback**: `ableton_clip` stop / `song.stop_all_clips()` stop the Session clip but don't re-engage the Arrangement, and the canonical LOM recovery `song.back_to_arranger=0` via `setattr` (`handlers/probe.py:219-245` bare `setattr`) is **silently ignored by Live** (known LOM quirk). Net symptom: a greyed-out Arrangement clip, then "transport moving, no audio"; the only fix found was the GUI **Back to Arrangement** button. **Surfaced** dogfooding swell, auditioning a "Swell theme preview" Arrangement clip on track 24 silently overridden by a leftover firing Session clip ("ZZ Theme Preview 1", slot 9). **Directions (defer to methodology on chunking):**
  1. **First-class recovery action** `ableton_session(action='back_to_arrangement')` — investigate the LOM call control surfaces actually use to clear the latch (may be state-dependent / a different entry point); if none reliable, surface a **teaching error** naming the GUI button + the override state rather than failing silently.
  2. **A real "locate and play" action** `ableton_session(action='play', from_bar=…/from_beat=…)` — today `seek`+`play` doesn't honor the located position: `seek(bar=243)` sets `current_song_time` but `play` restarts from the Arrangement Start Marker (~beat 8) and `continue_playing` resumes from the last stop. Root cause is Live's Start-vs-Continue-vs-start-marker distinction (the GUI works because the click moves the start marker). Move the start marker (or set `current_song_time` *and* use whichever play call honors it) so an agent can audition a section/preview deterministically; at minimum document the semantics in `ableton://guides/conventions`. RELATED but distinct: PSH-3H8M (perform watchdog) and ING-2S7K / ING-9H2T (automation scrub-read) also manipulate `current_song_time`/seek.
  3. **Guard the preview workflow** — when a Session clip is firing on a track that also has Arrangement content (or after `duplicate_to_arrangement`, cf. ARR-6T8N), warn that the Session clip will override the Arrangement, and/or stop+disarm the Session clip as part of the preview-to-arrangement handoff.
  4. **In-band teaching** (cross-cutting theme: these surfaces should TEACH in the tool result like `device set_sidechain`/`gain_db` already do, not return misleadingly-successful/silent results) — `ableton_clip stop` / `stop_all_clips` add a `still_overridden: true` flag + note; `play`/`continue_playing` add a `started_from` field + note; reading `song.back_to_arranger==true` (e.g. in `session` info) surface as a named latched state with the recovery, not a bare bool. The report has concrete copy the maintainer can lift for each.

  **Workaround (no in-song fix needed):** click the GUI **Back to Arrangement** button, then Play; optionally delete the leftover Session clip so it can't re-hijack on a future launch. **Verifiable signal:** an MCP gesture re-engages Arrangement playback on an overridden track (or a teaching error names the GUI recovery); `seek`+`play`/the new from-bar play deterministically sounds the seeked section; the firing-Session-clip-over-Arrangement case warns/guards; the listed surfaces return teaching messages instead of bare `ok`/bool. **Server-side** (`hallucinote_mcp` handlers — `probe.py`, `handlers/session.py`/transport, `handlers/clip.py`; in the MCP `_FINGERPRINT_PATHS`) ⇒ flips the fingerprint, all Live-gated to verify. (session-override recovery dogfood, swell, 2026-06-18)

  **PROGRESS (fix/mcp-reliability-papercuts, 2026-06-18):** the **deterministic core** is built (unit-tested, full MCP suite green) — direction 1 `ableton_session(action='back_to_arrangement')` (best-effort latch clear + `re_enable_automation` on the main thread, honest read-back, `cleared`/`was_latched`/teaching `warning` when Live ignores the clear — root cause of the silent-ignore confirmed as a LOM property quirk, *not* threading: probe `set` already runs on main via `run_on_main`); direction 4 in-band teaching across `session info` (`back_to_arranger` + `arrangement_override` block), `clip stop` (`still_overridden` + note), and new `play`/`continue_playing` (`started_from` + Start-vs-Continue note); plus the `ableton://guides/conventions` write-up. **DEFERRED (Live-gated — needs a running Ableton to design the mechanism, would be guessing without it):** (a) direction 2's composite `play(from_bar=…)` — the building blocks shipped (`continue_playing` + the documented `seek`+`continue_playing` recipe), but whether `continue_playing` honors a programmatic `current_song_time` set, vs. needing a Start-Marker move, must be verified in Live before a one-call action is added; (b) direction 3's preview-workflow guard (detect firing-Session-clip-over-Arrangement and warn/disarm) — the override-detection logic needs validation against real session state. Split these two into a follow-up Live-gated item at PR/merge time.

  **SHIPPED (2026-06-18):** CORE merged via **PR #183** (squash commit `f7164cb` on develop) — the `back_to_arrangement` action, in-band transport teaching, and the conventions guide are live. A **Critic-driven correction** went in with the merge: the transport teaching now reports the **verb actually invoked** (not a fabricated `started_from`), and redirects the "transport moves, no audio" case to the `back_to_arranger` latch — matching the Live-verified `render.py` behavior. **Two Live-gated halves split into follow-up MCP-3D6Q** (the composite locate-and-play action + the preview-workflow guard).
